# Old-design inventory: every page, ranked for redraw (24 Nov 2026)

> **Read-only.** This file changes no app code. It answers the owner, 23 Nov: *"check which pages still hold old
> designs that need to be redesigned."* Later redesign builders use §3 (what to redraw first) and §4 (who owns
> what, so two builders don't touch one file). The controller uses §0 and §6.
>
> - Measured on `main` @ `7021d15d2` (24 Nov 2026), against the specs corpus @ `0871b46` (24 Nov 2026).
> - Standards read: corpus `BUILD_RULES.md`, `HOUSE_DESIGN_STANDARD_2026-11-20.md` (Parts A–K, including Part I of
>   23 Nov and Part K of 24 Nov), `HOUSE_TEMPLATES.md` (T1–T37), `DARK_MODE_STANDARD_2026-11-20.md`,
>   `MOTION_STANDARD_2026-11-18.md`. The corpus wins on any conflict with this file.
> - It builds on [`audits/part-i-2026-11-23.md`](../audits/part-i-2026-11-23.md) (the Part I sweep, by file) and reads
>   its hand-checked lines; this file turns the picture into one row per page.
> - Re-measure any time: `node scripts/audit/old-design-inventory.mjs --md build-sessions/OLD-DESIGN-INVENTORY-2026-11-24.md`
>   refreshes §2–§5 in place and keeps the prose. The method is in §1.

---

## 0 · The answer

**476 pages.** 46 current · 167 partly old · 182 old · 81 redirect only (they forward, so there is nothing to draw).

Of the 395 pages that draw something, about 1 in 9 is current, 4 in 10 are partly old, and almost half are old.

| Role | Pages | Current | Partly old | Old | Redirect only |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Supplier | 89 | 6 | 34 | 31 | 18 |
| Couple (host) | 147 | 12 | 40 | 73 | 22 |
| Guest | 42 | 2 | 20 | 19 | 1 |
| Coordinator | 2 | 0 | 2 | 0 | 0 |
| Public pages, doors and sign-in | 77 | 15 | 26 | 32 | 4 |
| Admin | 119 | 11 | 45 | 27 | 36 |
| **All** | **476** | **46** | **167** | **182** | **81** |

*(§2 is the generated copy of this table; if they ever differ, §2 is the newer measurement.)*

**What "old" means here.** A page is **old** when it breaks four or more of the visible rules, or three with ten or more
breaks, or it was drawn before the house kit (hand-made or `.button-primary` buttons and no `ActionButton` anywhere it
draws: 53 pages). It is **partly old** when the house layout is there but some old pieces remain. It is **current** when
no visible rule breaks and at most five token slips remain. §1.4 has the exact rule.

## 1 · How this was measured

### 1.1 · What a page is

Every `page.tsx` under `apps/web/app`: 489 files. Left out: the `/dev` labs (`page.lab.tsx`, exempt by guardrail D17),
10 intercepting views `(.)x` (a second view of a page, not a page) and 3 parallel-route slots (`@rise/page.tsx`: part
of a layout). That leaves **476**. Route, area and doors come from the generated Ugat screens map
(`apps/web/lib/ugat/screens.generated.json`); admin pages from `apps/web/lib/admin-map/admin-routes.generated.ts`.
12 admin detail pages are in neither map and are still listed.

A page is **redirect only** when the screens map marks it a stub, the admin map marks it a redirect, or its file is a
short `redirect()` with nothing to draw.

### 1.2 · What a page draws

A page is judged on everything it draws, not only its own file:

- the page file, its `loading.tsx`, the other files in its folder and every private folder (`_components`, `_lib`, …) under it;
- the private folders of its group-less twin, for a page that moved into `(shell)` or `(hub)` and left its sections behind;
- every `.tsx` and `.css` file under `app/` it imports, up to four hops.

`components/**` and `lib/**` are the house primitives and are judged once, in §0.1, not on every page.
A file drawn by **3 pages or fewer** belongs to those pages. A file drawn by **4–12 pages** is an *area template* (the
marketing landing kit, a chat card): it renders on each page, so it counts on each, and the row names it as "via", because
one fix there moves them all. A file drawn by **more than 12 pages** is a *house piece*, listed in §4.3 and fixed once.

Approved product art is not counted (as in the Part I audit): invite themes, badge and award art, the gift box, monogram
and wax-stamp stages, cover scenes a couple picks, print designs, colour wheels, device mock-ups. On the guest Event Hub
(`/[slug]/*`, `/sample`, `/u/*`) colour, type, radius, gradient and dark are the couple's own theme (CLAUDE.md §3) and
are not counted; its controls, loading, layers and stripes still are.

### 1.3 · The rules and what the scan looks for

Comments are removed first with the repo's one lexer (`apps/web/scripts/port-controls.mjs` `stripComments`), so prose
about a banned thing is not counted as the thing. Where the repo already keeps a guard's baseline, the baseline is read
as it is, so this file and the guard agree.

| Rule (corpus) | Visible? | What is counted | Source of the count |
|---|:---:|---|---|
| **Part I·1** no decorative gradients | yes | hand-read gradient lines | `audits/part-i-2026-11-23.md` Rule 1 tables (✔) |
| **H3 🚫** never a coloured side stripe | yes | `border-l-[2-8]` / `border-s-*` with a colour, `borderLeft: Npx` | this scan (grey quote bars counted apart, not in the verdict) |
| **Part I·2** no icon tiles | yes | hand-read icon tiles | Part I audit Rule 2 (✔) |
| **Part I·3** one hero metric | yes | equal KPI rows | Part I audit Rule 3 (✔) |
| **Part I·4** hairlines, not shadows | yes | hand-typed frames and static shadows | `lint:no-card` baseline + Part I audit Rule 4 and Appendix B |
| **D2 · T19** one button primitive | yes | `.button-primary`, `.btn-*`, a `<button>` hand-filled in the accent | this scan (the public kit's `.m-btn` is not legacy) |
| **K1** one main button per scope | yes | an `ActionButton tone="brand"` that is not `main`; two `main` in one file | this scan |
| **D2 · D3 · T15** one input primitive, choices in a dropdown | yes | `.sn-input`, `shopInputClass`, raw `<select>`, raw typed boxes | this scan + `lint:field-template` baseline |
| **K3** one shared backdrop | yes | a hand-made `fixed inset-0` dimmer; `aria-modal` outside the shared pieces | this scan (the `one-backdrop-everywhere` guard already holds most) |
| **F1** Ghost skeleton, no spinner | yes | `animate-spin`, `Loader2`, `animate-pulse` | this scan |
| **B5 · D2** sort is `SlidersHorizontal` | yes | other Lucide sort or filter icons | this scan |
| **D2** search is an icon until tapped | yes | an always-open search `<input>` with no `search-add` / `search-box` / `ThumbRow` | this scan |
| **D3** no confirm dialogs | yes | `confirm(` | this scan |
| **B4 · K2** dark is part of done | yes | `bg-white` and `text-black` (they never flip; white words on a photo and black video grounds are not counted), text pairs under 4.5:1 in dark | this scan + `label-contrast-dark` baseline |
| **CLAUDE.md §3** one masthead | yes | a hand-rolled masthead | `page-masthead-baseline.json` |
| **Law 4 · B3** colour by job | no (tokens) | raw hex, `rgb(`, numbered palette classes | this scan + `design-rules.baseline.json` |
| **B5** the type scale | no (tokens) | `text-[Npx]`, `fontSize:`, CSS `font-size: Npx` | this scan |
| **B6** the radius scale | no (tokens) | `rounded-[…]`, `rounded-card`/`-tile`, `--sn-r-*`, CSS `border-radius: Npx` | this scan |
| **E1 · E2** motion tokens | no (tokens) | raw ms and easings, `transition-all` | `scripts/motion-raw.baseline.json` + this scan |
| **Part I·5** exact words | no | filler copy, vague periods | Part I audit Rule 5 (✔) |

### 1.4 · Verdict, size, seen

- **Verdict.** *Visible rules* are the rows marked "yes" above; *tokens* are the rest.
  - **current**: no visible rule breaks, and five or fewer token slips.
  - **old**: four or more visible rules break; or three with ten or more breaks between them; or the page is *pre-kit*
    (hand-made or legacy buttons and no `ActionButton` anywhere it draws).
  - **partly old**: everything else.
- **Size** of the work, from the page's **own** code (files only it draws; area templates are fixed once):
  - **S**: under 600 lines, under 25 breaks, at most two visible rules;
  - **L**: over 3,000 lines, or over 150 breaks, or eight or more visible rules;
  - **M**: the rest.
- **Seen.** The repo holds no traffic numbers, so "most-seen" is a proxy from the Ugat map: **A** an entry page every
  person passes (`/`, `/[slug]`, `/login`, `/signup`, `/dashboard`, `/dashboard/[eventId]`, `/vendor-dashboard`,
  `/v/[slug]`) · **B** in its area's menu (nav registry, menu doors, or the admin menu source) · **C** three or more links
  point at it · **D** deep or rare. Within a tier, more doors rank higher. This proxy was not checked against real visits.

### 1.5 · How sure this is, said plainly

- ✔ rows were opened and read by a person (listed in §5 with ✔; the reading notes are in
  `scripts/audit/old-design-inventory.notes.json` → `checked`). Every other number is a **candidate** from the scan: a
  builder opens the line before changing it. The Part I audit's own ✔ lines are carried over as they were on 23 Nov.
- A static scan cannot see **layout parity with a drawing**, which motion was chosen, or how a page behaves at run time
  (K3 inertness, real dark contrast at the glow). Those need the drawing beside a 390 px screenshot, light and dark.
- A big page (Home, Guests, the maker) draws hundreds of files, so it nearly always reads "old". For those, §3's
  "Old parts" and §5's anchors say *which* parts: often the layout follows a recent drawing and the old parts are pieces
  inside it. Treat them as "fix the named pieces", not "start over", unless the drawing session says so.
- The K1 count reads `ActionButton` props only. A hand-styled second button in the accent colour is counted under D2 instead.
- Grey quote bars (`border-l-2 border-ink/15` on a quoted line) are not counted as the banned stripe; they may still
  need a look call.
- Nothing was run in a browser: this container has no `node_modules`. The scan is plain Node and imports no app code.

### 1.6 · Re-run

```bash
node scripts/audit/old-design-inventory.mjs --summary
```

```bash
node scripts/audit/old-design-inventory.mjs --md build-sessions/OLD-DESIGN-INVENTORY-2026-11-24.md
```

```bash
node scripts/audit/old-design-inventory.mjs --json /tmp/old-design.json
```

The second refreshes §2–§5 in place (between the GENERATED markers) and keeps every other line. The hand-kept inputs
(drawings on file, open PRs, reading notes, the §4.1 table) live in `scripts/audit/old-design-inventory.notes.json`.

## 3 · The ranked redraw list (most-seen first, by area)

`Seen`: **A** an entry page everyone passes · **B** in its area's menu · **C** three or more links point at it · **D** deep or rare. Within an area, rows are in "seen" order (menu weight, then the number of doors in the Ugat map). `Size`: the page's own code, see §1.4. `Drawing on file`: the newest drawing folder in corpus `prototypes/` for that page; **none** means a Fable drawing comes first (guardrail A1). `Open PR`: open on 24 Nov and touching that page's files: wait for it, or coordinate with its builder.

## 4 · Pages two areas share

### 4.1 · Routes two roles use (read by hand)

| Route(s) | Roles | How the second role gets there | Who should own the redraw |
|---|---|---|---|
| `/[slug]` and its sub-pages · `/dashboard/[eventId]/launch` · `/dashboard/[eventId]/website/*` | guest · couple | The couple builds and previews the Event Hub in the maker; the maker draws the same `app/[slug]/_components` the guest sees. | Guest Event Hub builder (the hub is the couple's theme; the maker's own chrome is the couple builder's) |
| `/v/[slug]` and `/v/[slug]/*` | public · couple · supplier | A couple finds and books from it; the supplier previews it from `/vendor-dashboard/shop` and `/vendor-dashboard/website`. | Supplier builder (SHOP PAGE REMASTERED outline, 23 Nov) |
| `/dashboard/[eventId]/*` | couple · coordinator · co-hosts | A booked coordinator accepts `/host/accept/[token]` and works on the couple's event pages as a member. | Couple builder; the coordinator's seat changes only what is gated, never the look |
| `/vendor-dashboard/clients/[eventId]` | supplier (on a couple's event) | The supplier's view of one booked event draws couple pieces: run of show, payment plan, appointments (`app/_components/appointments-section.tsx`). | Supplier builder; shared pieces in §4.2 go to whoever is named on that row |
| `/dashboard/[eventId]/messages/[threadId]` · `/vendor-dashboard/messages/*` · `/dashboard/[eventId]/vendors/[vendorId]/workspace` · `/samahan/chat/[threadId]` | couple · supplier · coordinator | One chat (Unichat): every `app/_components/chat-*` file draws on all of them. | ONE chat builder for all four (chat_plus_final_2026-11-21) |
| `/pay/[reference]` · `/receipts/*` | couple · supplier · guest | Couples pay event plans and suppliers; suppliers pay their plan; guests pay Papic and E-Gifts. | Payments builder (pay_maya_2026-11-21); payments go first in the owner's order |
| `/papic/*` · `/dashboard/[eventId]/studio/papic/*` · `/vendor-dashboard/on-the-day/live/[eventId]` | guest · couple · supplier | Guests capture; the couple runs the event pool; a photographer supplier shoots into the same pool. | Papic builder for `/papic/*`; the couple and supplier builders keep their own studio pages |
| `/` · `/dashboard` | public · couple | Home and Discover share one layout (`app/(hub)/layout.tsx`); a signed-in person lands on the same front door. | Front-door builder; PR #8239 (Unihome v5) is open on both |
| `/admin/vendors/*` · `/admin/payments` · `/admin/payment-options` | admin (drawing supplier and couple pieces) | The admin desk renders supplier and couple components (for example `vendor-direct-pay.tsx`) to check them. | Admin builder last, after the owner pieces it borrows are redrawn |

## 6 · Notes for the controller

These are things found while measuring. None was changed here.

1. **Most drawings on file predate the newest rules.** Part I (23 Nov: hairlines, no gradients, no icon tiles, one hero
   metric) and Part K (24 Nov: one main button per scope, the dark glow, one shared backdrop) came after almost every
   drawing in §3. A builder told to "build exactly the approved drawing" would rebuild K1 and K2 breaks. The drawing
   session's look pass (Part J.3: look calls are the drawing session's) should restate each drawing against Part I and
   Part K before its page is queued. The exceptions dated 23–24 Nov: `for_suppliers_redraw_2026-11-23`,
   `buy_event_services_one_place_2026-11-23`, `compare_and_budget_by_category_2026-11-23`,
   `supplier_store_graphs_badges_2026-11-23`, `toolkit_templates_2026-11-23`, `one_profile_page_2026-11-23`,
   `login_simple_dark_2026-11-24`, `for_coordinators_benefits_2026-11-24`.
2. **K2 needs a top block to exist.** `PageMasthead` renders only actions and a screen-reader `<h1>` (owner lock,
   21 Aug, in its file header), while B5 still asks for "one 30 px bold screen title" and K2 for a glowing top block. On
   most pages there is no visible top block to light. This is a look call, then one shared piece (§0.1 F1).
3. **Pages with no drawing on file** are marked **none** in §3. Guardrail A1: a visible change to those waits for a Fable
   drawing and the owner's OK; fixes to an already-drawn page are exempt.
4. **Admin is mapped twice.** The Ugat screens map leaves admin out on purpose and the admin map lists 107 destinations;
   12 admin detail pages (for example `/admin/users/[userId]`, `/admin/vendors/[vendorProfileId]`) are in neither. Not a
   design finding; noted because §1.4's "seen" proxy cannot rank them.
5. **No changelog fragment in this PR.** The task said to commit only this doc and helper scripts. `SPEC IMPACT: None`
   (an inventory; no ruling changed).
